### [141] [Twisted Yangians of types BI, CI, DI and Drinfeld type current relations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14692)
*Vidas Regelskis*

Main category: math.QA

TL;DR: 本文为BI、CI、DI型分裂对称对引入了扭曲Yangian的转置表示，基于此推导出Drinfeld型电流表示，建立了R矩阵表示与Drinfeld表示之间的同构，并得到了张量积分解和PBW基等结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了建立扭曲Yangian的R矩阵表示与Drinfeld表示之间的同构关系，并澄清这两种表示的内在联系。

Method: 引入由扭曲反射方程控制的转置表示；利用生成矩阵的高斯分解；在R矩阵框架内推导Drinfeld型电流表示。

Result: 给出了特殊扭曲Yangian和扩展扭曲Yangian的Drinfeld型电流表示，其中Serre关系以封闭的电流形式表述；建立了R矩阵与Drinfeld表示间的同构；得到了扩展扭曲Yangian的张量积分解以及Drinfeld生成元上的PBW基。

Conclusion: 证实了Lu、Wang和Zhang关于扭曲Yangian两种表示同构的猜想，并为相关代数结构提供了更清晰的理解。

Abstract: We study twisted Yangians associated with the split symmetric pairs of types BI, CI and DI. We introduce a new presentation of these algebras, which we call the transposed presentation, governed by a twisted reflection equation that interacts naturally with the Gaussian decomposition of the generating matrix. Working entirely within the $R$-matrix presentation, we derive Drinfeld-type current presentations of the special twisted Yangian $SY^{\mathrm{tw}}(\mathfrak{g}_N)$ and of the extended twisted Yangian $X^{\mathrm{tw}}(\mathfrak{g}_N)$, in which the Serre relations are stated in a closed current form. Extracting coefficients recovers the Drinfeld presentation due to Lu. As a consequence, we establish the isomorphism between the $R$-matrix and Drinfeld presentations of these twisted Yangians conjectured by Lu, Wang and Zhang. As a byproduct, we obtain a tensor product decomposition of $X^{\mathrm{tw}}(\mathfrak{g}_N)$ into the twisted Yangian in the Drinfeld presentation and a polynomial ring in countably many central variables. We also obtain Poincaré-Birkhoff-Witt bases in the Drinfeld generators and describe the coideal coproduct on the low Drinfeld modes.

</details>


<div id='hep-ph'></div>

# hep-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [142] [Candia-v2: Logarithmic expansions for DGLAP evolution in $x$-space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14151)
*Casey Hampson,Marco Guzzi*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: Candia-v2是一个开源软件包，将x-space Candia算法推广到QCD的N³LO精度，高效求解非极化核子部分子密度的DGLAP演化方程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将原有的Candia算法从现有精度推广到N³LO（次次次领头阶）精度，以满足高能物理中对更精确部分子分布函数（PDF）演化计算的需求。

Method: 采用对数展开技术，在x-space中求解DGLAP演化方程；算法可系统性地扩展到QCD的所有阶数；新版本用更高效的实现替代了原C语言版本，并改进了API接口。

Result: 成功实现N³LO精度下的DGLAP演化求解；代码效率显著提升；API得到改进；软件以GPLv3许可证在GitHub上公开发布。

Conclusion: Candia-v2为高精度QCD部分子演化计算提供了高效、可扩展的开源工具，推动了微扰QCD计算能力的发展。

Abstract: We present Candia-v2, an open-source software package that generalizes the $x$-space Candia algorithm to next-to-next-to-next-to-leading order (N$^{3}$LO) accuracy in Quantum Chromodynamics (QCD). The code solves the DGLAP evolution equations for unpolarized nucleon parton densities using a highly efficient logarithmic expansion technique that can be systematically extended to all orders in QCD. Candia-v2 supersedes the previous original C implementation of the algorithm with significantly increased efficiency and improved API. The software is publicly available on GitHub under the GPLv3 license.

</details>


### [143] [New approach to the estimation of the lowest boundary of an axion mass](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14156)
*Sevinj O. Huseynova,Vali A. Huseynov,Nigar E. Musazade,Sevinj Y. Rzayeva*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过分析电子在恒定均匀磁场中的能谱（考虑反常磁矩），结合氢原子超精细结构中的三重态-单重态跃迁，理论估计了轴子质量的下限为5.885微电子伏特，并指出约10微电子伏特量级的预期值可通过太阳黑子中电子的轴子发射来解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 确定轴子质量的最低边界，以约束轴子模型并解释太阳轴子的可能来源。

Method: 理论分析电子在恒定均匀磁场中的能谱（考虑反常磁矩），将结果应用于氢原子电子（处于质子库仑场和质子磁矩产生的磁场中），利用超精细结构分裂中s能级三重态-单重态跃迁施加质量下限约束。

Result: 轴子质量应大于5.885微电子伏特；轴子发射所需的最小磁场强度由轴子质量决定；预期轴子质量下限约为10微电子伏特量级。

Conclusion: 轴子质量下限约为5.885微电子伏特，且太阳黑子中电子发射的轴子可解释约10微电子伏特量级的预期值。

Abstract: In this work, we have performed the theoretical estimation of the lowest boundary of an axion mass. We have analyzed the energy spectrum of the electron in a constant homogeneous magnetic field, taking into account its anomalous magnetic moment. We have applied the obtained results on the energy spectrum of the electron in a constant homogeneous magnetic field with allowance for its anomalous magnetic moment to the hydrogen atom electron, which is located in addition to the Coulomb field of the proton, as well as in the magnetic field produced by the magnetic moment of the proton. We have applied the idea of the restriction for the lowest boundary of the axion mass to the triplet-singlet transition between two adjacent levels obtained as a result of the hyperfine structure splitting of the s-levels in the hydrogen atom. We have concluded that the mass of an axion ought to be more than 5.885 microelectron-Volt. We have determined that the minimum value of the magnetic field strength at which the axion emission by the electron in a magnetic field starts to be realized is determined by the axion mass. This result enables us to estimate the expected value of the lowest boundary for the axion mass (of the order of 10 microelectron-Volt), assuming that some part of the solar axions is emitted by the electrons located in sunspots.

</details>


### [144] [Dark matter in composite Higgs models with a scotogenic EFT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14210)
*Yu Chen,Werner Porod*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了一类具有费米子UV完备的复合希格斯模型，该模型能解释暗物质遗迹密度。低能理论类似于scotogenic模型，残余Z2对称性使一个伪南布-戈德斯通玻色子稳定，成为暗物质候选者。以SU(6)/Sp(6)陪集为例，该模型有四个暗物质候选者，但仅三个能产生与观测一致的遗迹密度。通过MCMC研究探索有效理论参数空间，发现自旋-1共振在大部分参数空间中起重要作用，并讨论了LHC上的可能信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 寻求一种既能解释电弱对称性破缺（复合希格斯机制）又能同时解释暗物质遗迹密度的统一理论框架，将暗物质问题与复合希格斯模型的UV完备化相结合。

Method: 基于SU(6)/Sp(6)陪集的复合希格斯模型，利用残余Z2对称性稳定暗物质候选者；采用MCMC（马尔可夫链蒙特卡罗）方法对有效理论参数空间进行采样，重点研究暗物质可观测量；分析自旋-1共振对遗迹密度的影响。

Result: 模型存在四个可能的暗物质候选者，但仅三个能产生与观测一致的遗迹密度；自旋-1共振在大部分参数空间中起重要作用，影响暗物质湮灭过程；给出了LHC上可能的实验信号。

Conclusion: 复合希格斯模型可自然统一解释电弱对称性破缺与暗物质问题，自旋-1共振是暗物质产生机制的关键组成部分，该模型类具有可检验的LHC信号特征。

Abstract: We discuss a class of Composite Higgs models with a fermionic UV completion that can explain the dark matter relic density. The resulting low-energy theory resembles so-called scotogenic models. A residual $\mathbb{Z}_2$ symmetry implies that one of the pseudo-Nambu-Goldstone bosons is stable and therefore is a viable dark matter candidate. As a concrete example, we take a model based on the $\mathrm{SU}(6)/\mathrm{Sp}(6)$ coset, which in principle contains four dark matter candidates. However, only three of them can produce a relic density consistent with observations. We perform a MCMC study focusing on dark matter observables to explore the available parameter space of the effective theory. In particular, we find that spin-1 resonances play an important role in a substantial part of the parameter space. Finally, we comment on possible LHC signatures of this model class.

</details>


### [145] [Searching for the $N$-naturalness tower of neutrinos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14243)
*Sofia Lonardi,Manuel Ettengruber,Philipp Eller*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文首次通过全球中微子数据分析了N-自然性模型的中微子塔，报告了参数空间(r, N)的排除限制，并发现Majorana中微子情况下N≤10⁴、r≥0.1的区域被排除，这意味着M_GUT处的规范与引力统一基准场景被实验排除。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: N-自然性模型作为等级问题的潜在解决方案，引入了多个暗扇区中变化的希格斯质量参数。该模型需要参数r来编码扇区放置的精细调节程度。作者希望通过中微子实验数据检验该模型的可行性。

Method: 利用公开可用中微子数据进行全局分析，考虑中微子为Majorana或Dirac粒子两种情况，以及正常顺序和反顺序两种质量顺序，对模型参数空间(r, N)进行排除限制。

Result: 在Majorana中微子情况下，N≤10⁴且r≥0.1的参数区域被排除。特别地，N=10⁴（对应M_GUT处的规范与引力统一基准场景）被中微子实验排除。

Conclusion: 中微子实验数据对N-自然性模型给出了严格约束，排除了该模型的一个重要基准场景，表明该模型可能面临实验挑战。

Abstract: We present the first experimental search for the $N$-naturalness tower of neutrinos using a global analysis of publicly available neutrino data. As a potential solution to the hierarchy problem, the $N$-naturalness model employs a varying Higgs mass parameter among $N$ dark sectors and encodes the required fine-tuning of the placement of the sectors in a parameter $r$. We report exclusion limits on the parameter space ($r$, $N$), for normal and inverted ordering and in case the neutrino is Majorana or Dirac. In the case of a Majorana neutrino, we can rule out $N\leq 10^4$ sectors with $r\geq 0.1$. This is particularly exciting as it shows that a gauge and gravitational unification around $M_{GUT}$, which is a benchmark scenario of the $N$-naturalness framework with $N=10^4$ without fine-tuning, is ruled out by neutrino experiments.

</details>


### [146] [Constraining the Coexistence of Primordial Black Holes and Particle Dark Matter with Neutrino Observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14253)
*Prolay Chanda,Sagnik Mukherjee,James Unwin*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了原初黑洞（PBH）与粒子暗物质共存时，从高能中微子观测中获得的约束。通过改进暗物质晕轮廓和红移演化的处理，考虑了冻结、冻结-进入以及玻尔兹曼抑制的冻结-进入暗物质模型，并基于IceCube事件灵敏度和弥漫通量上限给出了约束，同时结合U(1)_{L_μ-L_τ}规范中介模型讨论了带电轻子与中微子关联末态的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究原初黑洞与粒子暗物质共存时，高能中微子观测对两者丰度的约束能力，以改进对暗物质组分和原初黑洞比例的联合限制。

Method: 采用改进的暗物质晕轮廓和红移演化处理，考虑冻结、冻结-进入和玻尔兹曼抑制的冻结-进入暗物质模型；利用IceCube事件灵敏度以及外推弥漫通量上限给出保守约束；并在U(1)_{L_μ-L_τ}规范中介模型下讨论中微子与带电轻子关联末态的影响。

Result: 给出了理想化的IceCube事件灵敏度以及基于弥漫通量保守上限的约束，展示了不同暗物质模型下的限制强度，并指出粒子物理完备模型通常会引入带电轻子与中微子的关联末态，影响约束的适用性。

Conclusion: 原初黑洞与粒子暗物质共存时，高能中微子观测能提供有效的联合约束，且粒子物理模型的完备性对约束解释至关重要。

Abstract: Primordial black holes (PBH) with a uniform mass scale could contribute up to 1\% of the gravitationally inferred dark matter relic abundance and remain consistent with observational limits over a large range of masses. In this case, the vast majority of the dark matter relic abundance is comprised of dark matter particles, such as WIMPs or FIMPs. Particle dark matter gravitationally captured around primordial black holes can form dense minispikes in which the annihilation rate is strongly enhanced. In this work, we investigate the constraints on the coexistence of PBHs and particle dark matter from high-energy neutrino observations. Relative to earlier analyses, we refine the treatment of the dark matter halo profile and its redshift evolution. We consider models of freeze-out and freeze-in dark matter, as well as Boltzmann-suppressed freeze-in. We present idealized IceCube event-based sensitivities together with conservative limits obtained by requiring that the predicted extragalactic neutrino intensity not exceed the upper envelope of the measured diffuse flux. We explore the constraints in terms of an idealized model with 100\% branching to neutrinos, we also discuss these results within the context fo a motivated gauged U(1)${}_{L_μ-L_τ}$ mediator model, emphasizing that a consistent particle-physics completion generally predicts correlated charged-lepton and neutrino final states.

</details>


### [147] [Collinear Factorization Violation and Reggeization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14259)
*Damiano Barcaro,Anjie Gao,Jonathan R. Gaunt,Aditya Pathak*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文推导了在空间类共线极限下，单个胶子被钉扎在Glauber区域的振幅的全阶软-共线子图分解，展示了Regge化行为，并通过避开Glauber区域简化了有效理论子图，开发了新软件工具以系统研究多圈水平的Glauber交换过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解并简化涉及Glauber交换的多点振幅中共线因子化破缺项的复杂计算问题。

Method: 通过全阶推导软-共线子图分解，利用变形避开Glauber区域简化有效理论子图，并开发新的软件工具辅助多圈计算。

Result: 实现了空间类共线极限下Glauber区域单胶子振幅的Regge化描述，显著降低了共线因子化破缺项的计算复杂度。

Conclusion: 为多圈水平上含Glauber交换过程的系统性研究奠定了基础，提供了新的理论框架和软件工具。

Abstract: We derive an all-order soft-collinear subgraph factorization in the space-like collinear limits of amplitudes for single gluon pinched in the Glauber region. We show that these contributions exhibit Reggeization, combining aspects of both forward and hard scattering. By deforming away from the Glauber region wherever possible we demonstrate a dramatic simplification of the effective theory subgraphs and thereby drastically reducing the complexity in computing collinear factorization breaking terms in multi-point amplitudes. Our work is aided with development of new software tools initiating a systematic study of processes involving Glauber exchanges at a multi-loop level.

</details>


### [148] [Production Effects and Final-state Interactions in $π_1 \to 3π$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14300)
*D. Winney,S. Gonzàlez-Solís,M. Mikhasenko,Ł. Bibrzycki,C. Fernández-Ramírez,V. Mathieu,G. Montaña,A. Pilloni,L. Qiu,A. Rodas,A. P. Szczepaniak*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用Khuri-Treiman形式论，统一描述了π₁(1600)信号道中3π末态的产生效应和末态相互作用，通过对COMPASS自由等压线数据的全局拟合，提取了短程产生和Deck机制的贡献，并获得了总3π不变质量依赖关系，为未来提取π₁在ρπ道中的极点位置奠定了基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: COMPASS合作组利用“自由等压线”技术分析了π₁(1600)的3π信号道，实验上直接观测到了P波ππ子道中线形的修正，这些修正既来源于末态相互作用，也来源于Deck机制等产生过程。现有方法无法区分这两类贡献，因此需要建立一个统一的形式论来分离并量化不同产生机制的相对强度和复相位。

Method: 采用Khuri-Treiman形式论作为统一框架，该形式论以初始产生振幅为种子，同时保持低能幺正性、解析性和交叉对称性。将产生效应与末态相互作用纳入同一描述，并对COMPASS的π₁自由等压线数据进行全局拟合，得到π₁→3π衰变振幅作为衰变和产生变量函数的多维参数化。

Result: 成功区分了短程产生和Deck机制的贡献，获得了总3π不变质量依赖关系的参数化形式，为在ρπ道中提取π₁极点位置提供了关键输入。

Conclusion: ππ质量谱的精确线形可以确定不同产生机制在3π末态中的相对强度和复相位；所得到的全局参数化结果对未来精确提取π₁(1600)的极点位置至关重要。

Abstract: We investigate the $π_1(1600)$ signal channel of $3π$ in the $J^{PC} = 1^{-+} \,[ππ]_{1^{--}}π~P$-wave. This channel has recently been analyzed by the COMPASS collaboration using the ``freed-isobar" technique which provides direct experimental access to the lineshape modifications of the $P$-wave $ππ$ sub-channel arising from both final state interactions and production processes such as the Deck mechanism. We motivate a unified formalism combining production effects and final state interactions using the Khuri-Treiman formalism, which is consistent with low-energy unitarity, analyticity, and crossing symmetry, seeded by an initial production amplitude. We demonstrate that the precise lineshape of the $ππ$ mass spectrum can be used to determine the relative strength and complex phase of different production mechanisms contributing to the $3π$ final state. We conduct a global fit of the $π_1$ freed-isobar data set, yielding a multi-dimensional parameterization of the $π_1\to3π$ decay amplitude at COMPASS as a function of both decay and production variables. We identify the contributions from short-range production and the Deck mechanism and extract the total $3π$ invariant mass dependence which will be important for a future extraction of the $π_1$ pole position in the $ρπ$ channel.

</details>


### [149] [Exotic states with charm and/or strangeness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14389)
*A. Martínez Torres,Breno Agatão,Pedro Brandão,K. P. Khemchandani,Luciano M. Abreu,E. Oset*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文探讨了具有粲和/或奇异数的奇特态的性质，这些态可由两体或三体强子动力学解释，特别关注D1(2420)以及从n\bar 系统预测的三体态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究具有粲和/或奇异数的奇特强子态的性质，理解其生成的动力学机制。

Method: 通过两体或三体强子动力学分析，研究奇特态的产生机制。

Result: 介绍了D1(2420)以及一些从n\bar 系统预测的三体态的性质。

Conclusion: 这些奇特态可由两体或三体强子动力学生成，相关研究有助于理解强子谱结构。

Abstract: In this talk, I will discuss the properties of several exotic states with charm and/or strangeness. These states can be interpreted either as being generated from two- or three-hadron dynamics. In particular, I will focus on $D_1(2420)$ and some predictions of three-body states obtained from $n\bar

</details>


### [150] [Searching for long-lived ALPs with a laser-assisted optical dump](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14492)
*Tong Li,Haolong Wang,Man Yuan*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文重新审视了利用激光辅助光学束流装置（optical dump）探测质量在MeV到GeV范围内、长寿命轴子类粒子（ALPs）的方案。研究涵盖了ALP-光子耦合引起的Primakoff过程和ALP-电子耦合引起的类Compton散射，并探讨了两者之间的关联与相互作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长寿命ALPs的衰变长度较长，需要具有宏观衰变体积的实验设施进行探测，而高强度的束流dump实验是理想平台。光学dump装置利用高能电子束与高强度激光脉冲碰撞产生硬光子，为探测ALPs提供了新途径。本文旨在系统评估该方案对MeV~GeV质量范围ALPs的探测能力。

Method: 基于ALP的低能有效拉氏量，同时引入ALP-光子和ALP-费米子相互作用项。分别计算ALP-光子耦合主导的Primakoff过程和ALP-电子耦合主导的类Compton散射过程，并分析两者之间的关联与相互影响。

Result: 研究扩展了光学dump搜索的范围，涵盖了两种耦合机制，并揭示了Primakoff过程与Compton散射之间的关联性，展示了两种ALP耦合在光学dump实验中的相互关系。

Conclusion: 激光辅助光学dump实验是探测MeV~GeV质量范围长寿命ALPs的有效手段，两种耦合机制的联合分析有助于更全面地约束ALP参数空间。

Abstract: The feeble interactions of light axion-like particles (ALPs) render them long-lived. Probing long-lived ALPs therefore demands facilities with a macroscopic decay volume to match their potentially long decay lengths, such as high-intensity beam dump experiments. An optical dump setup was proposed by utilizing hard photons from the collision of a high-energy electron beam and a high-intensity laser pulse. In this work, we revisit the probe of long-lived ALPs with MeV$\sim$ GeV mass via a laser-assisted optical dump. We consider the low-energy effective Lagrangian for ALPs incorporating the ALP-photon and ALP-fermion interactions. The scope of optical dump searches is extended to both the ALP-photon coupling induced Primakoff process and the Compton-like scattering via the ALP-electron coupling. We also investigate the correlation between Primakoff process and Compton scattering, and exhibit the interplay of two ALP couplings in light of optical dump experiment.

</details>


### [151] [Spin-Resolved Decay of Axion-Like Particles into Electron--Positron Pairs in Strong Electromagnetic Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14558)
*Xiaodan Mao,Yue-Yue Chen,Pei-Lun He*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 研究轴子类似粒子(ALP)在强激光场中自旋分辨的电子-正电子对衰变，利用Baier-Katkov算符形式导出了包含有限ALP质量效应和轻子自旋自由度的解析速率，揭示了自旋分辨通道、自旋关联以及产生的电子-正电子对纠缠特性随场强和质量的变化规律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索强激光场中ALP衰变为电子-正电子对的自旋分辨过程，特别是有限ALP质量效应和自旋自由度对衰变速率及产物纠缠特性的影响，为强场ALP搜索提供可观测的自旋分辨谱和纠缠特征信号。

Method: 采用Baier-Katkov准经典算符形式和局域常数场近似，推导了包含有限ALP质量效应和轻子自旋自由度的紧凑解析衰变速率。

Result: 在无质量极限下自旋求和速率与光子诱导的电子-正电子产生率有相同的渐近标度关系；赝标量耦合产生独特的自旋分辨通道和自旋关联。有限ALP质量在真空阈值附近重组能谱，阈值以下产生纯场诱导的电子-正电子对，阈值以上通过真空和场辅助贡献的相干干涉产生自旋依赖的振荡调制。产物纠缠反映主导产生机制：弱场中近阈值处近乎最大纠缠且类似单重态；远离阈值时约化自旋态变为三重态类似，强场主导时纠缠度为1/2，真空衰变主导时变为可分离态。

Conclusion: 自旋分辨谱和纠缠度可作为强场ALP搜索中有限质量和阈值效应的特征信号。

Abstract: We investigate spin-resolved decay of an axion-like particle (ALP) into an electron--positron pair in an intense laser field. Using the Baier--Katkov quasiclassical operator formalism and the locally constant field approximation, we derive a compact analytic rate retaining finite ALP-mass effects and the lepton spin degrees of freedom. In the massless limit, the spin-summed rate has the same weak- and strong-field asymptotic scalings as the corresponding photon-induced pair-creation rate, while the pseudoscalar coupling induces distinct spin-resolved channels and spin correlations. A finite ALP mass reorganizes the spectrum across the vacuum threshold, producing purely field-induced pair creation below threshold and spin-dependent oscillatory modulations above threshold through the coherent interplay of vacuum and field-assisted contributions. The entanglement of the produced pair reflects the dominant production mechanism. Near the vacuum threshold in weak fields, the pair is nearly maximally entangled and singlet-like. Away from threshold, the reduced spin state becomes triplet-like, retaining a concurrence of \(1/2\) when strong-field production dominates but becoming separable when vacuum decay dominates. These results identify spin-resolved spectra and entanglement as signatures of finite-mass and threshold effects in strong-field ALP searches.

</details>


### [152] [Thermal Masses and Bubble-Wall Friction in Cosmological Phase Transitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14867)
*Carlo Branchina,Stefania De Curtis,Luigi Delle Rose,Alessio Notari,Giuliano Panico,Matthew Starbuck*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 论文研究了热质量对一阶宇宙学相变中气泡壁摩擦的影响，发现热质量抑制了红外规范玻色子的贡献，使壁速度主要由顶夸克摩擦决定，从而降低了摩擦计算对红外等离子体区域的敏感性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在玻尔兹曼方程方法中，红外规范玻色子在无质量近似下的贡献被人为放大，带来较大的不确定性，因此需要更精确地处理热质量效应。

Method: 在Liouville算子和碰撞积分中一致地包含热质量，对比有/无热质量时顶夸克和规范玻色子的源项与相互作用率变化，并在单扩充标准模型中进行演示。

Result: 热质量对顶夸克的源项和相互作用率有抵消效应，仅带来百分之几的变化；但强烈抑制了红外规范玻色子的贡献，将主导动量移至温度量级，使规范玻色子效应成为次主导。

Conclusion: 热质量降低了摩擦计算对红外等离子体区域的敏感性，壁速度接近仅由顶夸克摩擦得到的结果。

Abstract: Bubble-wall friction controls the dynamics of first-order cosmological phase transitions. In Boltzmann-equation approaches, a major uncertainty arises from infrared gauge bosons, whose contribution is artificially enhanced in the massless approximation. We study the impact of thermal masses by including them consistently in both the Liouville operator and the collision integrals. Thermal masses suppress the source term for out-of-equilibrium perturbations while also reducing interaction rates. These effects largely cancel for top quarks, giving only percent-level changes, but they strongly suppress the infrared gauge-boson contribution, shifting the dominant momenta to scales of order the temperature. As a result, gauge bosons become subleading and wall velocities are close to those obtained from top-quark friction alone. We illustrate this in the singlet-extended Standard Model. Our results show that thermal masses reduce the sensitivity of friction calculations to the poorly controlled infrared sector of the plasma.

</details>


### [153] [Possible hidden-bottom molecular pentaquarks from $P$-wave $Λ_bB^{(*)}/Σ_b^{(*)}B^{(*)}$ interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14913)
*Yu-Xin Wan,Rui Chen,Fu-Lai Wang,Qi Huang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文基于单玻色子交换模型和耦合道效应，系统研究了P波底-反底分子五夸克态（包括束缚态和共振态），预测了丰富正宇称隐藏底分子五夸克候选态谱，为LHCb和Belle II实验提供指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 系统研究由基态底重子（Λ_b, Σ_b^{(*)}）和基态反底介子（B^{(*)}）通过P波相互作用形成的隐藏底分子五夸克态，填补该领域理论系统研究的空白。

Method: 采用单玻色子交换模型，考虑耦合道效应，推导所有允许量子数下的有效势；求解耦合道薛定谔方程寻找束缚态解，并进行相移分析识别共振极点。

Result: 在I=1/2扇区发现多个弱束缚态及关联共振（尤其在Σ_b B^*和Σ_b^* B^*道）；在I=3/2扇区吸引力较弱但仍获得弱束缚态和共振态（如Σ_b^* B^*的J^P=3/2^+,5/2^+,7/2^+态）；高自旋分波（如^6P_J分量）凸显自旋-自旋和张量相互作用的重要性。

Conclusion: 提供了P波隐藏底分子五夸克态的系统谱学预测，为LHCb和Belle II的未来实验搜寻提供了清晰指导。

Abstract: In this work, we perform a systematic investigation of the hidden-bottom molecular pentaquark states, encompassing both bound states and resonances, which originate from the $P$-wave interactions between ground-state bottom baryons ($Λ_b$, $Σ_b^{(*)}$) and ground-state antibottom mesons ($B^{(*)}$). Adopting the one-boson-exchange model and including the coupled-channel effects, we derive the effective potentials for all allowed quantum numbers $I(J^P) = 1/2(1/2^+)$, $1/2(3/2^+)$, $1/2(5/2^+)$, $1/2(7/2^+)$, $3/2(1/2^+)$, $3/2(3/2^+)$, $3/2(5/2^+)$, and $3/2(7/2^+)$. We then solve the coupled-channel Schrödinger equations to search for the bound-state solutions and perform the phase-shift analyses to identify resonance poles. Our results reveal a rich spectrum of positive-parity hidden-bottom molecular pentaquark candidates. In the isospin $I=1/2$ sector, we find several loosely bound states and associated resonances, particularly in the $Σ_b B^*$ and $Σ_b^* B^*$ channels, where the coupled-channel dynamics plays an essential role in their formation. In the isospin $I=3/2$ sector, the attraction is generally weaker because of the isospin factors. Nevertheless, we still obtain the loosely bound states and resonances, such as the $Σ_b^* B^*$ states with $J^P=3/2^+$, $5/2^+$, and $7/2^+$. The prominence of high-spin partial waves, for instance the $^6P_J$ components, underscores the importance of the spin-spin and tensor interactions. Our predictions provide a comprehensive and systematic spectrum of the $P$-wave hidden-bottom molecular pentaquark states and offer clear guidance for future experimental searches at LHCb and Belle~II.

</details>


### [154] [A Precise Determination of the Shape of the Proton's Gluon Cloud from HERA data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14955)
*Tobias Toll,Nahid Vasim*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 基于HERA实验的H1和ZEUS数据，利用热点模型对质子小x区域胶子结构进行精确提取，发现胶子云由微扰高斯核心(0.105 fm)和非微扰指数晕(0.220 fm)组成，模型拟合优度χ²/ndf=0.77。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确提取质子在小x区域的胶子结构，通过独占J/ψ产生过程研究胶子云的空间分布形态。

Method: 采用热点模型在领先扭曲阶解析计算相干和非相干截面，对H1和ZEUS实验所有可用的独占衍射光产生数据进行全局拟合。

Result: 热点具有0.105(1) fm的微扰高斯核心和0.220(2) fm的非微扰指数晕，形状与xpom或热点数无关，有效Regge斜率α'ₑff=0.046(19)具有稳定能量依赖性。

Conclusion: 该模型对数据拟合极好(χ²/ndf=0.77)，成功揭示了质子胶子云的双组分空间结构，为理解QCD在小x区域的非微扰行为提供了精确约束。

Abstract: We show the results from a comprehensive study of the gluonic structure of the proton at small $x$ extracted from exclusive $J/ψ$ production at the H1 and ZEUS experiments at HERA. We present an analytical calculation of the coherent and incoherent cross sections in the hotspot model of the proton at leading twist. We fit the model to all available exclusive diffractive photo-production measurements, and extract a precise shape of the proton's gluon cloud. The model shows an excellent fit to data with $χ^2/{\rm ndf}=0.77$. We find that the hotspots have a perturbative Gaussian core of size $0.105(1)$ fm, surrounded by a non-perturbative exponential halo of 0.220(2) fm. This shape is independent of $\xpom$ or hotspot number. The fit also shows a remarkably stable energy dependence with $α'_{\rm eff}=0.046(19)$.

</details>


### [155] [Model-Independent and Data-Driven Extraction of the Photon Distribution Function at the Electron--Ion Collider](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14958)
*Cong Li*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于截面比反演的方法，用于在电子-离子对撞机（EIC）上提取分布函数。通过将卷积结构简化为乘法形式，定义了实验截面与微扰可算理论输入之比R，并建立了R与目标分布函数f的关系，同时考虑了软光子辐射、有限bin宽度和实验接收度等实际因素对理论输入构建的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的分布函数提取方法在处理卷积结构时面临计算复杂性和不确定性挑战，尤其是在EIC实验条件下需要考虑多种实验效应。本文旨在发展一种更直接、更稳健的反演方法，通过截面比来简化提取过程。

Method: 从一般因子化公式dσ=H⊗f出发，针对合适的轫致辐射过程和恰当选择的微分观测量，将卷积结构约化为乘法形式；定义实验截面与微扰理论输入之比R；建立R与目标分布函数f的解析关系；探讨在考虑软光子辐射、有限bin宽度效应和实验接收度时如何恰当地构建理论输入dσ_hard。

Result: 成功建立了截面比反演方法的理论框架，证明了在特定条件下卷积结构可简化为乘法形式，并给出了R与分布函数f之间的显式关系。同时，明确了在实际实验条件下（软光子辐射、有限bin宽度、接收度效应）构建理论输入dσ_hard的准则。

Conclusion: 该截面比反演方法为EIC上分布函数的提取提供了一种新颖且实用的途径，能够有效处理实验中的多种复杂效应，具有较高的应用潜力。

Abstract: In this work, we investigate a cross-section-ratio-based inversion method for extracting distribution functions at the Electron--Ion Collider (EIC). Starting from the general factorization formula $dσ= H \otimes f$, we show that, for an appropriate bremsstrahlung process and suitably chosen differential observables, the convolution structure can be reduced to a multiplicative form. On this basis, we define the ratio $R = \frac{dσ_{\mathrm{exp}}}{dσ_{\mathrm{hard}}},$ where $dσ_{\mathrm{exp}}$ is the experimentally measured differential cross section and $dσ_{\mathrm{hard}}$ is a perturbatively calculable theoretical input. We then establish the relation between $R$ and the target distribution function $f$. Finally, we investigate how the theoretical input $dσ_{\mathrm{hard}}$ should be constructed when soft-photon radiation, finite-bin-width effects, and experimental acceptance are taken into account.

</details>


### [156] [Gluon GTMDs in the exclusive electroproduction of heavy-quark pairs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14964)
*Mattia Bellotti,Daniël Boer,Cristian Pisano*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 论文在GTMDs框架下研究了胶子参与的核子上的重夸克对独家电产生过程，给出了结构函数与GTMDs和GPDs的关系，并引入了基于对称无迹张量的胶子-胶子关联矩阵分解方法，结果对未来电子-离子对撞机实验有参考价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩展已知的独家重夸克对电产生过程的表达式，建立GTMDs与GPDs之间的明确联系，并为未来EIC实验提供理论基础。

Method: 在pQCD领头阶和共线展开一阶近似下处理短距离部分；引入基于部分子动量k_T和动量转移Δ_T的对称无迹张量洛伦兹基，对胶子-胶子关联矩阵进行GTMD展开；从振幅层面的核子螺旋度态而非极化态定义GTMDs。

Result: 给出了非极化核子情况下包含所有方位角调制的结构函数在GTMDs和GPDs下的表达式；建立了GTMDs与核子螺旋度振幅的清晰对应关系，明确了非极化核子束可探测的螺旋度差和螺旋度翻转矩阵元的贡献。

Conclusion: 该研究为EIC上独家重夸克对电产生过程的实验分析提供了更完整的理论框架，其GTMD表示方法使物理可观测量的解释更加透明。

Abstract: We study exclusive electroproduction of heavy quark-antiquark pairs off nucleons in the framework of generalized transverse momentum dependent parton distributions (GTMDs) for gluons. The short-distance part of the process is treated at leading order in perturbative Quantum Chromodynamics and in first order in a collinear expansion, which allows identification with the description in terms of Generalized Parton Distributions (GPDs). For the results for the structure functions in terms of GTMDs and GPDs we consider only unpolarized (spin-averaged) nucleons, but include all possible azimuthal modulations that can arise. The presented results extend known expressions in the literature and are relevant for experimental studies of this exclusive process at the future Electron Ion Collider. Furthermore, we introduce a convenient decomposition of the gluon-gluon correlation matrix in terms of GTMDs, expanded in a Lorentz basis of symmetric traceless tensors obtained from the partonic momentum $k_T$ and the momentum transfer $Δ_T$. The adopted notation for the GTMDs relates to the nucleon helicity states at the amplitude level, rather than to polarization states of the incoming nucleon or of the gluons, which makes it more transparent which contributions from helicity difference and helicity flip matrix elements can be accessed with unpolarized nucleon beams.

</details>


### [157] [Relativistic corrections and high-energy resummation for exclusive heavy quarkonium photoproduction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14986)
*Maxim Nefedov*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文计算了高能求和系数函数在$O(v^2)$阶的修正，涉及$O(v^2 α_s^n \ln^{n-1}(x/ξ))$项。当使用Gremm-Kapustin关系以矢量介子质量和重夸克速度表达时，该修正可忽略，但能部分抵消$O(v^2)$修正在LO系数函数中的$μ_F$依赖性，提高预测鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提高对高能区重夸克偶素（矢量介子）在强子靶上单举光生过程的预测精度和鲁棒性。

Method: 计算了高能胶子求和（小$x$区域）中系数函数的$O(v^2)$修正（$O(v^2 α_s^n \ln^{n-1}(x/ξ))$项），并使用Gremm-Kapustin关系将修正表达为物理的矢量介子质量$M_V$和重夸克速度平方期望值$\langle v^2 \rangle$的函数。

Result: 当以物理质量$M_V$和$\langle v^2 \rangle$表出时，$O(v^2)$修正（涉及高能求和）在标度$μ_F=M_V$处数值可忽略；该修正能部分抵消$O(v^2)$项对LO系数函数中$α_s$的$μ_F$依赖性，改善理论预测的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 高能求和中的$O(v^2)$修正虽数值小，但有助于稳定理论预测对重整化/因子化标度的依赖性，提高光生矢量重夸克偶素过程的理论可靠性。

Abstract: The $O(v^2)$ correction to the high-energy resummed coefficient function for the exclusive photoproduction of vector ($1^{--}$) heavy quarkonia off hadrons is computed, thereby taking into account corrections of $O(v^2 α_s^n \ln^{n-1}(x/ξ))$. When expressed in terms of the physical vector-meson mass ($M_V$) and the relative heavy-quark velocity ($\langle v^2 \rangle$) using the Gremm-Kapustin relation, the computed correction turns out to be negligible at the scale $μ_F=M_V$. However, it partially cancels the $μ_F$ dependence of the $O(v^2)$ correction to the LO coefficient function in $α_s$, thereby improving the robustness of the predictions.

</details>


### [158] [Radiative corrections in neutral-current (anti)neutrino elastic scattering at $\text{GeV}$ energies I: Nucleon targets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15050)
*Yi Chen,Oleksandr Tomalak,Bing-Song Zou*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在有效场论框架下引入了GeV能量级中性流（反）中微子-核子弹性散射的辐射修正，将截面因子化为软函数和硬函数，阐明了（反）中微子味道依赖性，改进了低能中性流过程的QCD贡献。单核子水平的辐射修正幅度与奇异夸克贡献相当，并与BNL E734和MiniBooNE实验数据高度吻合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在中性流（反）中微子-核子弹性散射中，辐射修正对精度测量至关重要，但以往处理不够系统。本文旨在利用有效场论框架系统地引入辐射修正，澄清味道依赖性，并改进QCD贡献，以提高理论预测精度并与实验数据对比。

Method: 采用有效场论框架，将散射截面因子化为软函数和硬函数；在振幅和截面两个层面分析（反）中微子味道依赖性；改进低能中性流过程的QCD贡献；将计算结果与BNL E734和MiniBooNE实验数据进行对比验证。

Result: 单核子水平的辐射修正幅度与奇异夸克贡献相当；理论计算结果与BNL E734和MiniBooNE实验数据高度一致。

Conclusion: 本文建立的中性流（反）中微子-核子弹性散射辐射修正框架是有效的，辐射修正不可忽略，且该框架能很好地描述现有实验数据。

Abstract: We introduce radiative corrections in neutral-current (anti)neutrino-nucleon elastic scattering at $\text{GeV}$ energies within the effective field theory framework. We factorize cross sections into soft and hard functions, clarify the (anti)neutrino flavor dependence at both amplitude and cross-section levels, and improve the quantum chromodynamics (QCD) contributions to low-energy neutral-current processes. The radiative corrections at the single-nucleon level reach a magnitude comparable to the contributions from strange quarks. We also compare our results with the experimental data from BNL E734 and MiniBooNE collaborations, finding excellent agreements with the experimental data.

</details>


### [159] [Inelastic Scattering Effects on Attenuation of Boosted Dark Matter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15098)
*Guanhua Gu,Liangliang Su,Lei Wu,Jin Min Yang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文将共振散射纳入地球衰减框架，结合弹性和非弹性散射通道，研究其对加速暗物质通量的影响，并利用PandaX-4T数据给出自旋无关的加速暗物质-核子截面的新约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地球衰减效应对解释加速暗物质的直接探测约束至关重要，因为与地球原子核的散射会显著改变到达地下探测器的通量和能谱。在加速能量下，非弹性核通道（如准弹性散射、深度非弹性散射和共振散射）可能变得重要，因此需要将这些过程纳入地球衰减框架以更准确地约束暗物质。

Method: 本文将共振散射与弹性、准弹性和深度非弹性散射通道相结合，系统地纳入地球衰减框架，分析重介质区中不同散射过程对加速暗物质衰减的贡献。

Result: 在重介质区，共振散射对加速暗物质的衰减有不可忽略的贡献。利用最新的PandaX-4T数据，得到了自旋无关的加速暗物质-核子截面σ̅_n的新约束。

Conclusion: 共振散射在地球衰减过程中起重要作用，将其纳入分析可以更准确地约束加速暗物质与核子的相互作用截面。

Abstract: Earth attenuation is crucial for interpreting direct-detection constraints on boosted dark matter (DM), since scatterings with terrestrial nuclei can significantly modify the flux and energy spectrum reaching underground detectors. At boosted energies, inelastic nuclear channels beyond ordinary elastic scattering can become relevant, including quasi-elastic scattering, deep-inelastic scattering, and resonant scattering. In this work, we incorporate the resonant scattering of boosted dark matter (DM) off nuclei into the Earth-attenuation framework, in combination with the elastic, quasi-elastic, and deep-inelastic channels. We find that, in the heavy-mediator regime, resonant scattering can give a non-negligible contribution to the attenuation of boosted DM. Using the latest PandaX-4T data, we derive new constraints on the spin-independent boosted DM-nucleon cross section $\barσ_n$.

</details>


### [160] [$B \to X_{\mathrm{s}} l^{+} l^{-}$ in the $μ$ from $ν$ Supersymmetric Standard Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15108)
*Xiao-Jie Hu,Hai-Bin Zhang,Tai-Fu Feng*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在μνSSM框架下研究新物理对稀有衰变B→Xs l+l-的影响，分析了主导Wilson系数贡献及其粒子来源，通过参数扫描验证与实验数据的兼容性，并揭示了前后不对称性中C7C10和C9C10干涉项的主导作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索μνSSM框架下新物理对稀有衰变B→Xs l+l-的修正效应，寻找超出标准模型的新物理信号。

Method: 对相关参数空间进行系统扫描，计算Wilson系数及其主导贡献，纳入B̅→Xsγ、Bs0→μ+μ-和125 GeV希格斯玻色子实验约束，并对前后不对称性进行干涉分解分析。

Result: 识别出主导Wilson系数贡献及其对应粒子；发现C7C10和C9C10干涉项是控制低q²和高q²区域前后不对称性的主导因素。

Conclusion: μνSSM框架下的新物理贡献与现有实验数据一致，前后不对称性的干涉结构揭示了可观测新物理效应的关键来源。

Abstract: We investigate the impact of new physics on the rare inclusive decay $B \to X_{\mathrm{s}} l^{+} l^{-}$ within the framework of the $μ$ from $ν$ Supersymmetric Standard Model ($μν$SSM). The dominant contributions to the relevant Wilson coefficients and their corresponding particles are identified and analyzed. By performing a systematic scan over the relevant parameter space, we elucidate the underlying physical mechanisms governing these dominant contributions and demonstrate their consistency with the experimentally allowed regions. Experimental constraints from the decays $\bar{B} \to X_{\mathrm{s}}γ$, $B_{\mathrm{s}}^{0} \to μ^{+} μ^{-}$, and the $125\,\text{GeV}$ SM-like Higgs boson are also incorporated. We perform a systematic interference decomposition of the Wilson-coefficient contributions to the forward-backward asymmetry, identifying the $C_7C_{10}$ and $C_9C_{10}$ interference terms as the dominant contributions governing its behavior in both the low- and high-$q^2$ regions.

</details>


### [161] [Spacelike-Collinear Scattering by the Method of Regions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15126)
*Wen Chen,Einan Gardi,Rourou Ma,Yao Ma,Yang Zhang,Zehao Zhu*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用区域方法研究规范理论散射幅的类空间性共线极限，发现了一种隐藏区域，该区域导致了分裂振幅对非共线部分子动力学的依赖，并解释了类空与类时限幅之间分析连续性的破坏机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释类空间性共线极限下分裂振幅违反严格共线因子化的机制，特别是其动力学依赖性的起源，并阐明类空与类时限幅为何在解析上不连续。

Method: 采用区域方法对五点点幅进行渐近展开，系统识别隐藏区域，并在超杨-米尔斯理论中计算隐藏区域对完整基积分的贡献，结合动量空间中的软和Glauber圈动量分析。

Result: 发现隐藏区域是类空间性共线极限中特有的，在类时极限中不存在；隐藏区域贡献恢复了精确的动力学依赖的因子化破坏分裂振幅；动量空间中隐藏区域由软和Glauber圈动量刻画。

Conclusion: 隐藏区域是连接交叉相关渐近极限解析连续性的断裂机制；Wilson线计算能捕获完整的动力学依赖性，解释了该现象在规范理论中的普适性。

Abstract: We study the spacelike-collinear limit of gauge-theory scattering amplitudes using the Method of Regions. The corresponding splitting amplitude violates strict collinear factorisation through its dependence on the non-collinear partons. While the associated colour dependence has long been known, starting at two loops the splitting amplitude also acquires dependence on their kinematics. We show that this kinematic dependence originates from a unique hidden region present in the asymptotic expansion of the five-point amplitude in the spacelike-collinear limit, but absent in the timelike limit. More generally, we propose that hidden regions provide the mechanism by which crossing-related asymptotic limits cease to be analytically connected. We develop a general algorithm for the systematic identification of hidden regions. Applying it to the five-point amplitude in super Yang-Mills theory, we compute the hidden-region contributions to the complete set of basis integrals and recover the exact kinematically dependent factorisation-violating splitting amplitude. In momentum space, the hidden region is characterised by soft and Glauber loop momenta. This explains why the Wilson-line calculation captures the complete kinematic dependence, thereby accounting for the observed universality across gauge theories.

</details>


### [162] [Quantum spin correlations in $Z^\prime$-mediated $t\bar{t}$ production at future lepton colliders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15153)
*ShivaSankar K. A.,Arindam Das,Sanjoy Mandal*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了未来轻子对撞机中顶夸克对产生过程中的量子自旋关联，在包含$Z^\prime$玻色子的$U(1)_X$扩展标准模型框架下，分析了自旋密度矩阵、纠缠标记、并发度、纯度及CHSH参数等量子信息可观测量，揭示了电子束极化对$U(1)_X$模型中左右手轻子电荷的直接探测能力，证明了量子自旋观测能为手性中性规范相互作用搜索提供互补信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索超越标准模型的中性规范玻色子（$Z^\prime$）对顶夸克对产生过程中量子自旋关联的影响，并评估量子信息可观测量在鉴别不同$U(1)_X$电荷分配方案中的敏感度。

Method: 在$U(1)_X$框架下，构建包含$γ$、$Z$和$Z^\prime$交换及其干涉的自旋密度矩阵，针对$\ell^+\ell^-\to t\bar t$过程，计算纠缠标记$\mathcal{D}_{\min}$、并发度、纯度及最大CHSH参数等量子信息可观测量，并考虑电子束极化效应。

Result: 不同$U(1)_X$电荷分配方案对量子自旋可观测量有显著影响；电子束极化可直接探测左右手轻子电荷；在$Z^\prime$共振区和极化碰撞中，量子信息观测量的行为与传统截面和角分布互补。

Conclusion: 量子自旋可观测量在搜索超越标准模型的手性中性规范相互作用方面，能提供传统可观测量之外的互补信息，电子束极化是区分不同$U(1)_X$模型的重要工具。

Abstract: We study quantum spin correlations in top-quark pair production at future lepton colliders in the presence of a neutral gauge boson from anomaly-free general $U(1)$ extensions of the Standard Model. The process $\ell^+\ell^-\to t\bar t$, with $\ell=e,μ$, is analyzed through the spin-density matrix including $γ$, $Z$ and $Z^\prime$ exchange and their interference. We focus on quantum-information observables such as the sufficient entanglement marker $\mathcal{D}_{\min}$, concurrence, purity and the maximal Clauser-Horne-Shimony-Holt (CHSH) parameter, and compare their behavior with conventional rate information. Within the $U(1)_X$ framework, we consider several representative charge assignments to investigate how different chiral structures influence these observables, with particular emphasis on the $Z^\prime$ resonance region and polarized $e^-e^+$ collisions, where the two allowed initial-state helicity configurations can be selectively enhanced. We show that electron-beam polarization provides a direct handle on the left- and right-handed lepton charges of various $U(1)_X$ scenarios. These results demonstrate that quantum spin observables provide information complementary to cross sections and angular distributions in searches for chiral neutral gauge interactions.

</details>


### [163] [Massive On-shell Splitting Functions in Spinor-Helicity Formalism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15191)
*Yi-Ning Wang,Chao Wu,Jiang-Hao Yu*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文基于Soper-Weinberg共线旋量，提出了一个用于大质量共线分裂函数的在壳构造形式体系，通过光前伽利略子群分解动量和旋量，系统推导了标准模型粒子在领头阶和次领头阶的所有大质量分裂函数，并建立了与无质量振幅的匹配词典。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的在壳螺旋度方法虽成功给出了无质量QCD分裂函数，但缺乏一个能够系统性地纳入有限质量效应的完整在壳构造方案，这限制了高精度对撞机物理中对大质量粒子（如顶夸克、W/Z玻色子、Higgs）的精确描述。

Method: 基于Soper-Weinberg共线旋量，利用光前伽利略子群（Poincaré群子群）的变换性质；将大质量动量和旋量按固定光样矢量n和n̄分解，使对齐区域m < p_T ≪ p_+的展开显式化；将领头阶结构与无质量三点振幅匹配，通过沿n̄方向添加Higgs动量探测次领头阶旋量分量，进而与无质量四点振幅关联；通过递归自举关系和伽利略对称性导出的普适替换规则得到高阶分裂函数。

Result: 推导了标准模型所有粒子完整的大质量共线分裂函数（领头阶和次领头阶）；建立了无质量与有质量耦合系数在三点和四点层次上的系统匹配词典；证明了高阶分裂函数可通过递归自举关系与普适替换规则获得。

Conclusion: 该形式体系可自然推广至有效场论算符和更高阶微扰修正，为精密对撞机物理和部分子簇射开发提供了灵活的计算工具。

Abstract: Collinear splitting functions govern parton evolution, parton showers, and resummation at high-energy colliders. While on-shell spinor-helicity methods have successfully yielded massless QCD splitting functions, a complete on-shell construction for massive particles, systematically incorporating finite-mass effects, is less developed. We present an on-shell constructive formalism for massive collinear splitting functions based on Soper-Weinberg collinear spinors, whose transformation properties follow from a light-front Galilean subgroup of the Poincaré group. Decomposing massive momenta and spinors with respect to fixed lightlike vectors $n$ and $\bar n$ makes the expansion in the alignment regime $m<p_T\ll p_+$ manifest. The leading-order structures are matched to massless three-point amplitudes, while an additional Higgs momentum along $\bar n$ probes the subleading spinor components and relates them to massless four-point amplitudes. We derive the complete set of leading and subleading massive splitting functions for all Standard Model particles and establish a systematic matching dictionary between massless and massive coupling coefficients at both the three- and four-point levels. Higher-point splitting functions are obtained through the recursive bootstrap relation with a universal substitution rule as a consequence of the Galilean symmetry. This constructive framework extends naturally to effective field theory operators and higher perturbative orders, providing a flexible computational tool for precision collider physics and parton shower development.

</details>


### [164] [Next-to-next-to-leading order QCD corrections to pion (kaon)-induced exclusive Drell-Yan process](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15214)
*Yu Jia,Bernard Pire,Qin-Tao Song,Guang Tang,Zhe-Yu Wang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文计算了π介子和K介子诱导的独有Drell-Yan过程的NNLO QCD修正，发现修正显著且为正，对精确理论预测与未来J-PARC实验数据比较至关重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: J-PARC未来实验将提供高能π和K介子束流，可用于研究独有Drell-Yan过程（即逆深度虚介子产生过程），需要精确的理论预测以便与实验数据进行对比。

Method: 在广义部分子分布（GPD）因子化框架内，在广义Bjorken极限下（Q² ≫ |t|, Λ²_QCD），计算了π⁻p → γ*(→ l⁺l⁻) + n 和 K⁻p → γ*(→ l⁺l⁻) + Λ 过程的NNLO QCD修正，精度达到领头扭度。

Result: NNLO QCD修正是显著的且为正，表明其对理论预测的可靠性至关重要。

Conclusion: 包含NNLO修正对于获得可靠的理论预测、与未来实验数据进行有意义的比较是必不可少的。

Abstract: The high-energy pion and kaon beams proposed for future experiments at J-PARC offer a unique opportunity to investigate exclusive Drell-Yan processes induced by pions or kaons, which correspond to inverse deeply virtual meson production with $M=π,K$. To facilitate precise comparisons between theoretical predictions and forthcoming experimental data, we calculate the next-to-next-to-leading order (NNLO) QCD corrections to the processes $π^- p\to γ^*(\to l^+l^-) + n$ and $K^- p\to γ^*(\to l^+l^-) + Λ$. Our calculations are performed within the generalized parton distribution (GPD) factorization framework, accurate to leading twist in the generalized Bjorken limit ($Q^2\gg |t|,\,Λ_{\rm QCD}^2$). We find that the NNLO QCD corrections are substantial and positive; therefore, their inclusion is imperative for reliable theoretical predictions in confrontation with future experiments.

</details>


### [165] [Particle production from bubble collisions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15279)
*Anish Ghoshal,Pratyay Pal,Alessandro Strumia*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文指出，宇宙学相变中超相对论性气泡碰撞产生重粒子的传统方法（作为标量背景的离壳衰变）在参数上高估了硬粒子产生，且依赖规范选择和场空间坐标选择。作者提出了一种替代形式——类似于高能碰撞的部分子描述，将硬粒子产生视为洛伦兹收缩壁中量子之间的在壳散射，并应用于重标量、费米子和矢量粒子的产生，探讨了对暗物质、轻子生成、引力子产生和原初引力波的启示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纠正传统方法（标量背景离壳衰变）在超相对论性气泡碰撞中硬粒子产生问题上的参数高估和规范/坐标依赖性，建立更正确的理论框架。

Method: 提出类似高能碰撞部分子描述的形式：在超相对论极限下，碰撞气泡几乎自由穿过，硬粒子产生来自构成洛伦兹收缩壁的量子之间的在壳散射。

Result: 传统方法的离壳衰变模型参数上高估了硬粒子产生且存在规范/坐标依赖；新形式理论上更自洽。

Conclusion: 该部分子框架适用于重标量、费米子、矢量粒子产生，并对暗物质、轻子生成、引力子产生和原初引力波有重要启示。

Abstract: Collisions of ultra-relativistic bubbles during cosmological phase transitions can produce particles much heavier than the transition scale. Previous analyses modelled this process as the off-shell decay of the scalar background. We show that its results parametrically overestimate hard particle production and depend on the gauge choice and the coordinate choice in field space. We propose an alternative formalism, analogous to the partonic description of high-energy collisions. In the ultra-relativistic limit, the colliding bubbles undergo nearly free passage and hard production arises from on-shell scatterings among the quanta constituting the Lorentz-contracted walls. We apply this approach to heavy scalar, fermion, and vector particle production, and study the implications for dark matter, leptogenesis, graviton production and primordial gravitational waves.

</details>


### [166] [Decoherence and More Coherence in the Radiative Decay of the $Z$ Boson](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12015)
*Kun Cheng,Tao Han,Harman Singh,Youle Su*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文以Z玻色子辐射衰变为例，系统研究了标准模型手征相互作用下τ⁻τ⁺γ三体末态的自旋态，发现光子辐射对费米子对纠缠态的影响既可能导致退相干也可能单调增强纠缠。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 粒子对撞过程中的末态辐射可视为与未观测自由度的相互作用，常涉及纠缠态退相干问题。作者希望以Z玻色子辐射衰变为代表，定量研究光子辐射如何改变费米子对的量子信息属性。

Method: 基于标准模型手征相互作用的解析计算，对τ⁻τ⁺γ三体末态自旋态进行全相空间分析，利用多种量子信息可观测量量化光子辐射对τ⁻τ⁺自旋态的影响。

Result: 发现光子辐射对纠缠的影响具有双重性：在某些相空间区域导致退相干，在其他区域则单调增强费米子对的纠缠程度。

Conclusion: 辐射光子对量子关联的影响并非单一的退相干效应，而是与相空间区域相关的复杂现象，为理解高能物理过程中的量子信息演化提供了新视角。

Abstract: Final state radiation in collider processes can be interpreted as interactions with unobserved degrees of freedom and is often discussed within the context of decoherence of an entangled state. We consider the radiative decay of the $Z$ boson as a representative example and perform a detailed analytical study of the $τ^-τ^+γ$ spin state, as determined by the chiral interactions of the Standard Model, over the complete three-body phase space. We explore various quantum information observables to quantify how the $τ^-τ^+$ spin state changes with the photon radiation. We find some striking features, for example that the emitted photon could either lead to decoherence or monotonic enhancement of entanglement for the fermion pair.

</details>
